`hosts.findKeeperFor` first probes for a LIVE keeper child and ATTACHES (keeperSid) instead of second-writing. ②unreachable-host memo (60s) — transcript fetch serves stale cache instantly instead of a 15s ssh timeout per window. ③CRITICAL dial-bridge restore read meta under a cw-stripped name that NEVER matched → no dial session survived any server restart (fixed + bridge keyed by webui id). ④IMPLICIT FORK adoption — `claude --resume` of a conversation locked by another live claude silently forks to a new id; the 2.156.1 hijack guard vetoed it, so live stream showed turns that every rebuilt history lost ('compact recap里有,窗口不展示'); a first-id-line change on a RESUME spawn (`session._resumeSpawn` && !`_sawFirstId`) is now adopted. ⑤failed-resume rescue — resume closes the old window first, so a failed create's spec-less shell EVAPORATED on refresh (silent window loss); it now flips into view-only + real viewSession openSpec. ⑥`agentdSession`/`forkRequested` persisted+restored; ssh chat terminate is mechanism-AGNOSTIC (tries agentd state-file kill AND keeper stop — the restored-flag miss silently orphaned remote claudes). ⑦dead-socket cleanup preserves REMOTE sessions' metas as `.orphan` files (B-1525 first half). ⑧writeSessionMeta atomic; uncaughtException runs the full flush belt. ⑨reconnect refetches maintenance+settings; reattach safety timer 30s; unknown-prior-epoch reattach = full view reload; read-only windows skip reattach. ⑩(2.219.1) session cards surface `remoteState` — a LIVE card whose host is unreachable carries an amber 'host unreachable' chip (the local wrapper shell IS alive and queues input by design; the card no longer pretends the machine is fine); rides active-sessions, restored from wrapper meta.
- **Dead-session replay = blank window shell (2.217.0, userL ×12 real report; user directive: restart后必须能看read-only view+手动resume)**: attachSession builds its ChatView only INSIDE the one-time 'attached' handler — an initial-attach error (stale serverId replayed after the server lost its sessions: OOM kill, pod/docker recreation) had NO ChatView for the error path to flip read-only, so the handler dropped and the window stayed a bare shell. Fix: the error branch drives `_viewIntoWindow` (shared with viewSession) into the SAME window — view-only history + Resume bar; works host-less (the transcript finder scans data/remote-jsonl) and with the host machine DOWN (fetchSessionJsonl serves the stale cache instead of throwing). ChatView-side `_tryViewOnlyRescue` covers the reconnect-time variants. Root enabler fixed too: syncSessionIdentity read `match.hostId` but live entries carry `host` — remote sessions' specs NEVER got their host backfilled. Smoke: scripts/test-attach-rescue.mjs (worktree+CDP, 8 asserts).
- **Resume guard (2.179.0, userW's duplicate-session incident)**: a plain claude `--resume` REUSES the conversation id — resuming an id that is ALREADY live on the same machine put TWO claude processes on ONE JSONL (double-writer class) + duplicate sidebar cards (real incident: billing-switch kill no-op'd on a stale webui id, the respawn then double-resumed). ws-handler 'create' refuses (`code:'resume-already-live'` + existing session info; client attaches the live session instead); forks exempt (new id). **CODEX WAS NEVER EXEMPT (P1 codex double-writer, 2026-09): the guard's "codex resume forks a new thread id" clause was FALSE for the current wrapper — `thread/resume` REUSES the thread id (only `thread/fork` mints one), so resuming a thread still held by a live app-server put two app-servers on one rollout with no refusal, no sweep and no `_resumeWarning`. Now the guard matches codex on `backendSessionId` (same host semantics), the writer sweep has codex legs (see writer-sweep.js), and the client re-sends codex resumes on reconnect under the same guard-turns-duplicate-into-attach reasoning as claude.** The 'kill' case falls back to resolving by backendSessionId when the webui id is unknown, and the billing switcher passes it.
- **Username migration must also re-encode claude's project dirs (2.236.1, userW's real incident)**: the 3.5.0 image's vibe→<name> rename + `/home/vibe` symlink covers recorded ABSOLUTE paths, but claude encodes transcript dirs from the RESOLVED cwd — pre-migration transcripts under `projects/-home-vibe-*` were invisible to every resume ("No conversation found with session ID"). `migrateLegacyHomeProjects()` (server.js boot, before restoreSessions) renames each to the new encoding + symlinks the old name (VibeSpace's own cwdToProjectDir over recorded /home/vibe cwds resolves through it); idempotent, collisions skipped.
- **Changing the container USER ripples to EVERY `kubectl exec` (2.162.7 regression)**: the 3.5.0 personalized-username image runs the container as root (boot-root.sh renames vibe→instance-name, drops via runuser); but `kubectl exec` bypasses the entrypoint's runuser and runs as ROOT — so the admin's exec-based git update hit "dubious ownership" on the uid-1000 PVC repo and every update failed. Fix pattern: `git config --system --add safe.directory '*'` at boot (root) + `safe.directory $(pwd)` in update.sh. Any root-USER container change must audit all kubectl-exec-driven ops (update/restart/probes).
- **Dial-device (paired) sessions — FULLY FIXED + E2E-verified (B-0d70, 2.163.0–2.164.1; scripts/dbg-dial-session-e2e.mjs, real dialed daemon, 22 asserts)**: the failure stack, all fixed: (1) stale cached DeviceManager bound to a dead ws stream after re-dial — online=true but blank (`_dialStream` tracking, rebuild on change, cache invalidation on dial-in, 2.162.3); (2) daemon child spawns inherit launchd/systemd's minimal PATH → no node/claude (spawnEnv prepends node dir + tool bins, 2.162.4); (3) chat-wrapper only substituted `__VS_OFFSET__` when VIBESPACE_REMOTE_SID was set — dial's agentd-attach got a literal placeholder → offset=NaN → zero relay → blank (OFFSET_MODE gates on the placeholder's presence, 2.162.6); (4) **THE #1 blank-chat root cause (2.163.0)**: the server shipped ITS OWN os.homedir() (`/home/<user>` on the pod) as the device spawn cwd — on a Mac that path doesn't exist, and `child_process.spawn` with a nonexistent cwd emits an async `'error'` event that, WITH NO LISTENER, **crashed the whole device daemon → every session blank**. Fix: the daemon's pipe-session resolves cwd to a real dir (fallback $HOME) + attaches a spawn 'error' listener (a bad cwd/binary can NEVER take the daemon down); ws-handler defaults a remote/dial session's empty cwd to the DEVICE home (`hosts.homeDir`, dial-aware); RemoteFs `info`/`home`/`stat` + `_run` gained a device fast path so `/api/file/info?host=<dial>` works (was ssh-only → 400 "has no ssh" → New Session preflight reported every existing dir nonexistent = the "/Users/<user> 不存在" report). (5) **terminal-on-dial IMPLEMENTED (2.164.0)**: device runs claude/codex in a node-pty via the daemon's `open-session`, proxied through DialSessionBridge (pty mode: open-session/resize/kill + live bytes); local side = dtach → pty-wrapper → vibespace-agentd-attach in a new RAW-TTY mode (`stty raw` on its controlling tty — the exact `ssh -t` shape). node-pty best-effort installed by the pairing installer (chat/files/mounts never need it). **Terminal-on-dial invariants (2.164.1 review, do not regress)**: (a) the bridge KILLS the device pty on attach `onDead` + `close(sid)` — a pty is LIVE (not the keeper model), and pty-wrapper's REMOTE_RETRY respawns the attach on every link flap, so without the kill each reconnect orphaned another claude on the device; (b) attach-cli pty mode exits 0 on `session-exit`/permanent errors (auth/proto/session-error printed once) so pty-wrapper FINALIZES instead of looping 120× on a nonzero device-child exit — only a genuine transport death reconnects; (c) SIGWINCH reads `process.stdout.columns` in a `setImmediate` (a sync read races Node's own size refresh → forwards the STALE size); (d) terminal-dial billing mirrors the chat guard (reject an explicit subscription §ban-safety; fail the create on API-key placement failure — no silent wrong-billing). Codex CHAT on dial fails fast (JSON-RPC wrapper can't ride the byte pipe, B-0588); codex TERMINAL works. Diagnosis toolkit: pod-internal WS probe + read data/session-buffers/<id>.buf bytes + /proc/PID/cmdline for literal __VS_OFFSET__ + hosts/:id/test tools probe + a temp shared-file tracer for the SIGWINCH chain (server→pty-wrapper→attach-cli→bridge→daemon).
- **#workspace must be overflow:clip, NEVER a scroller (2.161.2, three-iteration live-tracer diagnosis)**: overflow:hidden containers are still PROGRAMMATICALLY scrollable — browser focus-scrolling scrolled the whole workspace whenever focus landed inside a window extending past its bottom (freeform windows legally can), with no scrollbar to undo it; every window then appeared shifted by exactly scrollTop ("整个工作区被往下推", stuck at 239px live). Correlated with bottom-edge right-clicks, masqueraded as a context-menu bug. clip + a scroll-snapback listener in WindowManager (belt). Diagnosis pattern: telemetry ring-buffer tracer on the user's machine; beware self-inflicted diff artifacts (windows keyed by a constant '?' fabricated the 'windows moved' evidence — key diffs by REAL ids).
- Permission/AskUserQuestion resolution must survive restarts WITHOUT the control_response (2.109.5, real report: an answered questionnaire resurrected as awaiting-input): the control_response only exists in server MEMORY — it goes to claude's stdin and the wrapper's .buf tees stdout only, so a restart-rebuilt history is always request-without-response. FOUR cooperating guards (keep all): (1) MessageManager's tool_result merge auto-resolves an unresolved `permission` (result ⇒ answered) and emits `permission` in the edit fields ONLY when newly resolved there (unconditional re-emit re-appended the "✓ Allowed" chip renderToolMsg deliberately omits on live completions + clobbered client selectedAnswers); (2) _processControlRequest's error→pending restore applies ONLY to interrupt-FLUSHED tools (content still tool_call) — a message with a REAL tool_result is settled and auto-resolves instead (else the [tool_use, tool_result, control_request] end-append replay order resurrected denied/errored cards), together covering BOTH replay orders (the buffer-merge anchor can place control_request before OR after the tool_result depending on rotation); (3) **is_error alone is NOT a denial** — `_resolutionFromResult` treats only the CLI's canned user-rejection text as denied (an APPROVED tool that then fails is is_error too — hundreds per transcript — and must not render "✗ Denied"); (4) `activePendingPermissions()` (session-store) scans ALL records for resolving tool_results, never a tail window (the answer scrolls out of any fixed window while the session keeps working — same class as the chatStatus 200→2000 scan-depth bug); plus the client attach injection skips tools already complete/error. Run-collapse side (same release): a card with an UNRESOLVED permission returns null from `_updateRuns`' kindOf (never folds — hidden Allow/Deny stalls the turn unnoticed) and the permission-edit path calls `_updateRuns()` directly (the overlay mutates the card in place; the childList MutationObserver never fires for it). **Paging compensation is ELEMENT-ANCHORED, never scrollHeight-delta (2.111.5, tracer-diagnosed root fix; COMPLETED 2.229.1 — the paging-jump recurrence had three residual holes, all forensics-confirmed via a scrollTop-write interceptor in scripts/test-chat-paging.mjs: ① _extendBottom's per-message _onCreateMessage path ran the PINNED live-trim storm per batch (now gated on !_loadingHistory — batch callers trim once); ② _trimTop still used relative `scrollTop -=` which DOUBLE-COMPENSATED against the browser's native scroll anchoring reacting to the same removals (captured ±4000px adjacent-frame oscillation; now absolute element-anchored, delta = anchorless fallback only — RULE: any scroll compensation must be an ABSOLUTE anchor restore, a relative ± collides with native anchoring); ③ _withViewportAnchor returned failure when its anchor got FOLDED by a run-collapse pass inside fn (likely since 2.213.0 widened foldable kinds) → callers hit estimate-skewed delta; now restores on the nearest visible sibling like _updateRuns)**: under content-visibility:auto a fresh insert measures at its ~80px ESTIMATE while trimmed elements had REAL heights — the delta can go NEGATIVE (captured live: insert-50/trim-50 shrank scrollHeight 312px → scrollTop clamped to 0 → top sentinel load-looped at the clamp). `_withViewportAnchor(fn)` (chat-view.js) wraps every paging mutation (_extendTop with the fold inside, _loadEarlierGap, _trimGapDom top-side); delta math survives only as the no-anchor fallback. Diagnosis pattern: chat scroll tracer (ring buffer of every scroll-affecting op + Ctrl+Shift+J manual dump → /api/telemetry kind:'trace' name:'chat-scroll-jump'; the wheel-recent exemption HID the repro — jumps happen mid-scroll; content-DISPLACEMENT jumps produce NO scroll event, so a 300ms viewport-top-drift watcher covers those). Run-collapse × virtual-scroll invariants (2.109.5, real report "往上翻阅跳动+翻不回来"): `_updateRuns` VIEWPORT-ANCHORS every pass (captures the topmost visible non-header child + offset before mutating, restores after — collapsing above-viewport content must never move what the user reads; skipped when pinned; a folded anchor falls forward to its next visible sibling); `_extendTop`/`_extendBottom` call `_updateRuns()` synchronously right after their own scroll compensation (the 180ms-debounced observer pass used to fold freshly loaded cards AFTER compensation → viewport yank → sentinel re-trigger → load loop); run open/closed memory (`_runExpanded`) is keyed by ANY member, not the first; while PINNED only the LAST run keeps an opened state (2.216.0 — an accidental/watch expand inherited into a growing run stayed open forever; unpinned reading never auto-collapses) (pagination prepends older members onto a run, changing its first element — a first-member key re-collapsed the run being read on every _extendTop); `_updateRuns` ends with `_runsObserver.takeRecords()` — the `_runsMutating` flag alone CANNOT suppress observer self-triggering (MutationObserver callbacks deliver at a microtask checkpoint after the finally resets the flag), so without the drain every pass scheduled another identical pass in a permanent 180ms rebuild loop.
- SILENT TRANSCRIPT LOSS (2026-07-03, CRITICAL): sessions spawned by a server that was itself started from INSIDE a Claude Code session never wrote lock files or project JSONLs — `CLAUDE_CODE_CHILD_SESSION=1` (inherited through dtach→pty-wrapper→claude) alone puts the CLI into child-session mode (verified by A/B env test on 2.1.199; `CLAUDECODE`/`CLAUDE_CODE_ENTRYPOINT`/`CLAUDE_CODE_SESSION_ID` individually are harmless). Conversations looked fine live but vanished on terminate+resume (WebUI and CLI both read the missing transcript). server.js now strips `CLAUDECODE`, `CLAUDE_CODE_*`, `CLAUDE_EFFORT`, and stale `CLAUDE_WEBUI_*` from process.env at startup when it detects it was launched inside a session, and restoreSessions warns (Linux /proc scan) about surviving sessions still running poisoned. NOTE for agents working on this repo: restarting the server from your own Bash is SAFE ONLY because of this sanitizer — do not remove it.
- Chat status bar empty until first reply after resume/attach: model + contextWindow came only from `result.modelUsage` / system-`init` records, which are stream-json **stdout-only and never written to the JSONL** — any pure-JSONL restore got `model:null, contextWindow:0`. Fix in `SessionMessages.chatStatus()`: fall back to `assistant.message.model` (skip `<synthetic>`), infer contextWindow from observed usage (>190k tokens ⇒ 1M beta else 200k); permission mode isn't in the JSONL at all → `session._permissionMode` (remembered from launch args) merged into chatStatus by both WS attach and `/api/session-messages?withStatus=1`. Codex needs none of this (rollout JSONL carries turn_context model + token_count + approval policy natively).
- Clipboard image paste failures: (1) `paste` event never fires — xterm.js v5 uses Clipboard API directly, bypassing paste event. Fix: `attachCustomKeyEventHandler` intercepts Ctrl+V, redirects focus to hidden contenteditable div. (2) `navigator.clipboard.read()` unavailable over HTTP. Fix: contenteditable div receives real paste event with clipboardData.items. (3) `xclip -i file` unreliable. Fix: `cat file | xclip` pipe. (4) Bracketed paste `\e[200~...\e[201~` inserts text, doesn't trigger image check. Fix: send raw Ctrl+V (`\x16`) which triggers Claude Code's Ink framework clipboard check. (5) Server polls `xclip -t TARGETS -o` to confirm clipboard ready before responding. (6) DISPLAY can NOT be trusted from the inherited env — servers (re)started from shells with a stale `DISPLAY=:99` broke every xclip call, and under XWayland even the right display fails without the compositor's XAUTHORITY cookie (`/run/user/<uid>/.mutter-Xwaylandauth.*` — note "Xwaylandauth" does NOT contain the substring "xauth", match /auth/i). `detectXDisplay()` (server.js) probes DISPLAY candidates (env + /tmp/.X11-unix/X*) × XAUTHORITY candidates (env, /run/user/<uid>/*auth*, ~/.Xauthority) with `xset q` at startup; the winning pair is used for the server's xclip calls (app.locals.xEnv) AND injected into session spawn env (the CLI reads the clipboard itself on Ctrl+V). Sessions spawned before the fix keep their dead DISPLAY until restarted. **COOKIE ROTATION (2.67.1, real incident):** a compositor/Xwayland restart mints a NEW `.mutter-Xwaylandauth.XXXXXX` file — every running session keeps the OLD path → "Invalid MIT-MAGIC-COOKIE-1", paste dead in all of them at once. Fix: `stabilizeXAuth` merges the working cookie into `~/.Xauthority` and spawns sessions with THAT stable path (processes re-open the auth file per X request, so a later `refreshXEnv()` merge heals everything without respawn); the paste route retries once through `app.locals.refreshXEnv` on cycle failure. One-time manual heal for pre-existing sessions: `xauth -f <oldfile> merge <newfile>`.
- Spontaneous terminal shrink + disconnect (mid-use, no user action): `resizeSessionToMin` min'd over ALL `session.clients`, but entries get injected at a hardcoded 120×30 placeholder on `attach` and on subagent View-Log attach (the viewer registers into the PARENT session's clients map). A reconnecting/ghost client at the placeholder, or an open subagent viewer, won the min and shrank the PTY for everyone (broadcast `effective-size` → `terminal.resize`). Compounded by NO WS heartbeat — a half-open connection (network blip, sleep/wake, OOM-induced unresponsiveness from heavy local jobs) lingered in the clients map for the full TCP keepalive window. Fix: (1) client entries carry `real:true` only after a genuine terminal `fit()` resize; `resizeSessionToMin` considers only real clients (falls back to non-viewer placeholders when none, for chat PTYs); subagent viewers flagged `viewer:true` and never participate. (2) WS heartbeat (ping every 30s, terminate on two missed pongs) evicts ghosts promptly. (3) Terminal re-`fit()`s on reconnect so the placeholder is replaced by a real size before the heartbeat could shrink to it.
- Dead settings in schema: removed 9 settings defined in `settings-schema.js` but never read by any code (enableAutoGrouping, enableStarredDrawer, showNewSessionCard, showRefitButton, showOverlapIndicator, flatTimeSort, suppressWaitingOnRestore, layoutBindings, colorOverrides). Removed empty Hotkeys, Themes, File Explorer categories.
- Tab merge hit-test matched occluded icons: dragging A's titlebar onto A's own position would merge with a stacked window B underneath (because `getBoundingClientRect` ignores occlusion). Fix: use `elementFromPoint` after temporarily disabling `pointer-events` on the dragged element; match the `.window` ancestor. Extracted into shared `_detectTabMergeTarget` helper on tab-group mixin, used by all three drag paths (titleBar, icon drag, tab drag-out). Side benefit: window.js was checking for `.tab-bar` (wrong class name — actual is `.tab-bar-tabs`), so tab-bar merge drops never worked; now they do.
- `/compact` leaving chat stuck on 'thinking': stream-json emits `user` messages (compact summary) but no `result` after `/compact`. `_processUser` never called `_finalizeStreaming`, so stale streaming assistant messages persisted indefinitely. Fix: `_processUser` now calls `_finalizeStreaming` at start of new user message. Wrapper also treats `compact_boundary` system message as end-of-stream (`meta.streaming = false`).
- isStreaming derived from stale normalizer messages: old heuristic scanned normalizer messages for `status==='streaming'`, but `processLive` could create streaming entries before `convertHistory` finalized them (race on server restart). Fix: replaced with explicit `session._isStreaming` flag tracked from protocol signals (Claude: `result`/`compact_boundary`/`user`; Codex: `task_started`/`task_complete`/`turn_aborted`/`task_failed`). Also set immediately on `chat-input` send (no round-trip delay). Initialized from wrapper metadata on restore, cleared on exit. Single source of truth — no more heuristic.
- Huge sessions (>512MB JSONL) — tail-only load + continuous seek scroll (no seam marker): `readFileSync(fp,'utf-8')` THROWS past Node's ~512MB max string length (swallowed error → blank chat). `readJsonlBounded` (codex adapter, shared with Claude session-store) with `{tailOnly:true}` loads a line-aligned last-32MB TAIL only — NO head, NO elision seam marker (the old head+2MB+seam+tail model is gone). The whole earlier history is one continuous virtual scroll: `ChatView._installSeekSentinel` puts an invisible 1px `.chat-gap-sentinel` at the top of the tail; an IntersectionObserver + scroll-driven `_maybeSeekEarlier` call `_loadEarlierGap`, which seek-reads one ~2000-record slab older each time (`/api/session-history-gap?endLine=N&count=C`), walking the cursor from `tailStartLine` down to line 0 (`_finishSeek` removes the sentinel at 0). Gap messages carry `.chat-gap-msg`, are excluded from virtual-scroll trim + `_windowStart/_windowEnd` accounting, render read-only (orphan tool results where the call is outside the slab — acceptable). Seek primitives: `getJsonlLineIndex` caches a line→byte-offset table (streaming Buffer scan, no >512MB string; EXTENDS incrementally — append-only growth scans only the new tail bytes, so seeks stay ~15ms even while a live session keeps writing, instead of re-streaming 100s of MB per jump); `jsonlGapInfo` gives `{tailStartLine, totalLines}`; `readJsonlLineRange` seek-reads any raw line range (records tagged `__line`). `?info=1` returns the gap; `?endLine`/`?startLine=N&count=C` seek-normalize a slab (subagent-filtered to match the display path); `&whole=1` unclamps the read to `[0,totalLines)` (default clamps to `[0,tailStartLine)` so continuous scroll-up never re-reads the registered tail). JUMPS (search + minimap) TELEPORT by ABSOLUTE FILE LINE — immune to normalized-index drift AND to the live tail sliding forward as the file grows. `ChatView._seekTeleport(line)` replaces the whole view with a `whole=1` slab centered on the line (±300 lines), keeps+resets the sentinel (scroll-up continues by line), sets `_teleported` + `_gapCursorDown`, and shows the scroll-to-bottom button as 'return to latest' (click → `jumpToBottom` restores the live registered tail, `_resetGapAfterJump` clears `_teleported`). Teleport browsing is BIDIRECTIONAL: scrolling down seek-loads newer slabs (`_maybeSeekLater`/`_loadLaterGap`, cursor `_gapCursorDown`, 3s idle backoff at file end) and the DOM is capped by `_trimGapDom(side, cap=3400, keep=2400)` which drops far-side elements and REWINDS the corresponding cursor so scrolling back re-loads them — the cap MUST exceed teleport slab + one full 2000-line slab (~1200 msgs) per direction or up/down loads thrash each other's content. content-visibility is force-disabled only while the jump LANDS (stable heights → sync scroll hits), then `_setStableHeights(false)` re-enables it ~1.6s later. The collapse of never-cv-rendered elements is ASYNC (relevancy decided at paint) — scrollHeight shrinks hugely + scrollTop clamps, so scroll-delta compensation FIGHTS native scroll anchoring (observed scrollTop yanked to 0; view relocated ~1.5s after landing). Correct approach: on restore, REPLAY the landing — `_scrollElStable(this._lastJumpTargetEl)` or the search's recorded `_lastRevealRun` (exact range inside a tall message), skipped if the user scrolled since the jump (`_lastUserScrollAt`). A match already RENDERED in the live view (|element ts − match ts| < 2s) scrolls directly — no teleport (replacing the live view for something on screen was jarring); same fast path in `_jumpToFileTime` via `_scrollToNearestTs(ts, 2000)`. The pos indicator hides while teleported (its window-index numbers are stale). The `.chat-search-flash` overlay parents inside the chat container (absolute, host-relative coords) NOT document.body — a body-level fixed overlay draws over other windows stacked above. One jump primitive: `jumpToFileMatch` (search) and `_jumpToFileTime` (minimap) both teleport, so there is NO index-drift path left. Landing is content-visibility-proof: `_scrollElStable` / ChatSearch `_revealNearest` re-center over ~12 rAF frames + timed re-centers at 180/400/750ms (heights keep computing for ~1s after a big slab loads); `_revealNearest`/`_revealInEl` route through `_scrollToRange`, which handles FOUR reveal blockers: (a) most tool cards are COLLAPSED `<details>` — `_expandRangeAncestors` opens the match's whole ancestor chain first (+ forces content-visibility on the message); (b) expanding fires the card's DEFERRED REHIGHLIGHT (`data-highlight-deferred`, toggle listener) which REPLACES the card DOM and detaches the Range — every settle step re-acquires a live range from the same (stable) `.chat-msg` element when `startContainer.isConnected` goes false; (c) `_scrollNestedIntoView` scrolls nested overflow ancestors on BOTH axes — vertical (max-height cards) AND horizontal (nowrap code lines; only when the match is outside the h-viewport, so wrapped text doesn't jiggle); (d) centers the outer list on the range nearest the anchor (raw-line→message isn't 1:1). It pulses a `.chat-search-flash` overlay glued to the range for ~1.2s (takes a GETTER so it follows a re-acquired range; hidden while clipped by the list or any inner scroll container); the current highlight is a solid high-contrast `::highlight(chat-search-current)` vs the dim `chat-search` and `applyHighlightLayer` re-asserts the current highlight from a stored node+offset `_currentAnchor` (scroll-driven re-applies would otherwise wipe it). Search covers the WHOLE file and STREAMS progressively (less-style live count): `searchJsonlFullStream` (codex adapter) reads the JSONL ASYNC (yields between 16MB chunks so the response flushes and other requests aren't blocked; raw-substring pre-filter → parse candidates → match extracted text; 500-match cap; 240MB ≈ 0.6s) via `?search=q&stream=1`, writing NDJSON: a `{tailStartLine,totalLines}` preamble, then one `{line, ts, role, preview}` per hit as found, then `{done,total,truncated}`. ChatSearch (`_streamFullFileSearch`) reads the stream, jumps to the FIRST hit immediately, updates the status `${idx}/${n}… searching` live, finalizes to `${idx}/${n}${truncated?'+':''}` on done, and aborts the prior stream (AbortController) on a new query. The `Cache-Control: no-transform` header makes the compression middleware skip buffering so writes flush in real time. (Non-streaming `?search=q` + sync `searchJsonlFull` kept as a fallback.) Matches carry `{line}`; all matches seek-load/teleport by absolute file `line` (no normalized index needed — the teleport model is immune to index drift). ChatSearch auto-switches to full-file streaming mode when the gap minimap is active. The MINIMAP covers the whole conversation: `scanJsonlUserTurns` (mtime-cached) streams the file once (substring pre-filter `"role":"user"` → parse ~2k candidate lines, ~1.2s for 541MB); `?fullturnmap=1` returns every user turn in TIME coordinates + file line; `ChatMinimap.renderFullExtent` positions markers by `(ts-firstTs)/(lastTs-firstTs)`, thumb tracks the visible ts span (`setVisibleTsRange`←`_reportVisibleTsRange`, which uses getBoundingClientRect for real visibility + min/max ts, and in teleport mode ignores ts past the conversation's `lastTs` — the normalizer stamps `Date.now()` on slab records lacking a timestamp, which would otherwise stretch the thumb to the recent end; live messages are also deferred while teleported so they don't leak into the history view); clicking a marker `_jumpToFileTime(ts, line)` teleports. Messages carry `srcLine` (from each record's `__line`) + DOM `data-ts`/`data-line`. NOTE: a live-growing session's tail slides forward, so tail-relative indices drift for the active session — teleport-by-line sidesteps this entirely.
- Codex thread vanished from session list permanently: a transient IO error in `extractCodexThreadMeta` was swallowed and the empty meta (threadId='') was cached keyed by the file's mtime — if the session never wrote again (terminated), the entry never invalidated. Fix: failed extractions are never cached.
- Stale openSpec replay → blank chat window with outdated title: a window whose dtach session died and was resumed under a NEW server id kept `serverId`/`name` from creation time in its `_openSpec`. `replayOpenSpec` replayed them verbatim (attach to nonexistent session → blank; stale first-user-message name instead of custom rename), `captureState` dropped `backendSessionId` when the stale serverId no longer matched `_allSessions` (self-perpetuating), and the server's attach-not-found error had no `sessionId` so the ChatView couldn't correlate it. Fix: replayOpenSpec re-resolves serverId/name against the live session list by backend+backendSessionId (+ `getCustomName`), falls back to `viewSession` when dead; captureState/restoreState/loadNamed fall back to `openSpec.backendSessionId`; error carries `sessionId` and ChatView goes read-only with the Resume bar.
- Chat drag-upload overlay permanently visible ("won't disappear"): **this project has NO global `.hidden {display:none}` — every component defines its own scoped rule**. The new `.chat-drop-overlay`/`.chat-upload-toast` had a base `display:flex` and toggled a `.hidden` class with no matching CSS, so they were never hidden. Fix: added `.chat-drop-overlay.hidden`/`.chat-upload-toast.hidden { display:none }`. Verification gotcha: assert `getComputedStyle(el).display`, NOT `classList.contains('hidden')` — the class toggled correctly the whole time while the element stayed `display:flex`. (Same root cause as the earlier minimap-label-not-hiding bug.) The overlay show/hide itself uses a dragover-idle timer (no `dragleave`/`relatedTarget` — unreliable in Safari — and no `dragend`, which OS/Finder file drags never fire).
- Claude fork behaved identically to resume in chat: the WebUI **never captured Claude's stream-json `session_id`** (zero refs anywhere — for new sessions the id was filled in lazily by lock-first discovery). `--fork-session` makes claude mint a NEW session id and write a separate JSONL (verified empirically: plain resume reuses the id, fork returns a fresh one), but the server kept tracking the PARENT id passed at creation — so a forked window shadowed the original (same name/history/resume target) and the fork's transcript was orphaned. Fix: the **claude** stdout parser (`setupSessionPty`'s chat branch is `if (backend==='codex'){…} else {…claude…}` — the adoption MUST go in the `else`; a first attempt in the codex branch silently never ran) adopts `msg.session_id` when it differs from the tracked id, records the parent in `forkedFrom` (informational for Claude — discovery never hides on it, unlike Codex), persists `claudeSessionId` in session meta, and `broadcastActiveSessions()`. Guarded by a one-shot `session._forkRequested` flag (set in ws-handler only when `data.fork` + Claude) so a normal resume — whose id the parser also sees on every line — can never be hijacked. **FIRST capture is UNCONDITIONAL since 2.156.1 (userL real incident): the fork guard used to veto null→id adoption — the ONLY parser-side capture — so a brand-new session could never learn its id from the stream; local sessions were silently rescued by lock-first discovery, REMOTE keeper sessions were not (meta null forever → attach transcript-prefetch path error → window permanently blank while the remote claude ran fine). With no tracked id there is nothing to hijack; a CHANGED id still requires the fork flag.** **Timing gotcha (why it looks like resume at first):** under `--input-format stream-json` claude emits NOTHING until the first input message — the fork id rides in the `system/hook_started`/init line that only appears AFTER the user sends their first message. So a freshly-forked window is genuinely indistinguishable from a resume until you continue it; on that first turn claude forks, the parser adopts the new id, and the window becomes its own session (separate JSONL, distinct sidebar entry, resumes as the fork). The client propagates the adopted id to the window `_openSpec` via `syncSessionIdentity` on the next `active-sessions` broadcast (same path Codex fork/resume uses). Needs a server restart to take effect (dtach sessions survive it). Verified live: fork + one message → `backendSessionId` 649e5dbe… → 5c480868…. **UX follow-up:** because the divergence needs a first message, clicking Fork (chat mode) now opens `#dialog-fork` (a "first message" popup); submit forks AND sends it (`createSession({initialMessage})` → `_sendChatMessage` → `chat-input`, sent AFTER history renders so `loadHistory` doesn't wipe the echoed message) so the session diverges immediately instead of mimicking a resume. Terminal-mode forks skip the popup. The popup also has an editable **Title** field (`#fork-title`, pre-filled with the default `<base> (forked)` via `_defaultForkName`) so you fork straight into the title you want; the title is passed as the session name (window title is right immediately) and — once the fork's NEW id is adopted (`syncSessionIdentity`, guarded by a remembered `parentId` so the parent isn't renamed before divergence) — persisted as a custom name (`sidebar.setCustomName`) keyed by the fork's session key. The window-title resolver prefers `getCustomName`, so a forked window keeps its real title instead of falling back to the first-message-derived name after it stops or the page reloads. **Fork-from-message (point-in-time fork):** each assistant message in chat shows a GitHub repo-forked icon (`UI_ICONS.forkBranch`) next to the open-in-editor button (`addForkBtn`, claude-only, assistant-only, not subagent views, requires `msg.uuid`). It branches a session truncated at that message via `claude --resume <id> --resume-session-at <message.uuid> --fork-session`. **Two CLI gotchas (found by disassembling the binary; the help text misleads on both):** (1) `--resume-session-at`'s help says "(use with --resume **in print mode**)" but it works fine in our stream-json interactive mode — verified live, no `--print` needed (which we must never use → program-use billing). (2) help calls the arg `<message id>` but it's looked up by **`message.uuid`** (the JSONL record uuid), NOT `message.id` (`msg_…`) — passing the latter errors `No message found with message.uuid of …`. So the normalizer now threads the raw `uuid` onto every message (next to `srcLine`). The forked window's displayed history is truncated to match via `/api/session-messages?untilUuid=<uuid>` (returns the convo up to that message, last 50 of that range, `total=upto` so scroll-up still paginates). Related discovered-but-unused: `--rewind-files <user-message-id>` restores **file** state to a message point (the rewind/checkpoint feature — separate from conversation fork). Verified live end-to-end: fork-at + message → new id adopted (2a1e29c6…).
- Minimap/search accuracy (huge sessions, reconnect, gap slabs) — five related fixes + the 2.286.1 sixth, keep ALL of them: **(6, incident-diagnosed via the auto-shipped scroll tracer) INDEX-mode jumpToIndex needs the SAME landing machinery as the teleport path** — it kept a single-rAF scrollIntoView, so on tool-heavy windows the 180ms-debounced run-collapse pass + cv height resolution yanked the landed view to scrollTop 0 (+ a spurious extendBottom on collapsed heights) ~200ms after landing; now folds runs synchronously pre-measure + lands via _scrollElStable. CDP smoke: scripts/test-minimap-jump.mjs (9 asserts, distinct-target-proven). (1) index→element mapping must use `'.chat-msg:not(.chat-gap-msg)'` — gap-loaded elements are in the DOM but OUTSIDE the window index space, so a bare `.chat-msg` NodeList misaligns `relIdx` onto the wrong message (search expanded/scrolled the wrong card once any gap slab was loaded). (2) `_reattach` catch-up must advance `_windowEnd` after appending missed messages (`_fetchMessages` silently updates `_total` via `_fetchMessagePage`, so the two diverge otherwise — post-reconnect minimap thumb/pos-indicator/jumps were off by the missed count); it also feeds missed user turns to the minimap and re-applies the search highlight. (3) The search highlight layer must be re-applied after EVERY window mutation: `_extendTop` AND `_extendBottom`/`jumpToIndex`/`jumpToBottom` (only the first did → highlights vanished scrolling down). (4) Full-extent (time-coordinate) minimap: live messages have ts beyond the init-time `lastTs` — `updateThumb` clamps against `max(lastTs, visibleBotTs)` (stale span pinned the thumb to top:100%, overflowing the track) and new live user turns call `appendFullTurn`. (5) Index-mode markers are `startIdx/total` — `setViewport` repositions them when total changes; edit re-renders must preserve `dataset.ts` (create path sets it, the edit path lost it).
- CodeMirror active-line highlight hid the selection on the first/last selected line: `highlightActiveLine` (from `basicSetup`) decorates the line under the selection HEAD even during a range selection, and that background paints on `.cm-line` ABOVE the selection layer (`z-index:-1`), so the opaque light-theme active-line (`#f1f5f9`) covered the selection rect on that one line. Fix: tag the editor `.cm-has-selection` when any range is non-empty + a theme rule `&.cm-has-selection .cm-activeLine{background:transparent}` (outranks light + oneDark `.cm-activeLine`). **Focus gotcha:** first attempt toggled the class directly on `view.dom` via a ViewPlugin — but CodeMirror rebuilds `view.dom.className` from scratch on every focus change (`updateAttrs` → `"cm-editor" + (cm-focused) + themeClasses`), wiping the class, so the highlight reappeared once the editor lost focus. Correct way: contribute the class through the **`EditorView.editorAttributes` facet** (a function value) — `attrsFromFacet` re-evaluates it on every update (incl. selection + focus, `updateAttrs` runs at the end of each view update) and `combineAttrs` CONCATENATES its class into the className CM builds, so it survives blur. Both editors share `activeLineSelectionPatch` (exported from code-editor.js). Lesson: never mutate `view.dom.classList` on a CodeMirror editor — it's clobbered on focus/theme changes; use the editorAttributes facet.

- **r6 integration (2.369.74, the r6 verifier's last finding):** `setupUsage()` runs at module top level and loads `data/usage-cache.json` into `_rateLimitCache` BEFORE `runLocalMigrations()` (inside `server.listen`'s callback), so a repair that unlinks the poisoned sibling changes nothing for that process — `usage-routes` now exports `reloadRateLimitCache()` (re-read + re-merge, idempotent) and server.js calls it right after the runner; test-readings-attribution §16c(h) drives the production order through the real route with the no-reload arm as the negative control. The migration ships under the id `2026-09-refile-readings-by-window-v2` because 2.369.73 marked the un-suffixed id applied as a DEFERRAL.

- **2026-09-08 (2.369.75 gate): `listOpenRolloutPathsViaLsof` budget 4 s → 20 s and a timeout is UNKNOWN, never `[]`** — lsof enumerates every process's fds before `+D` filters, and on a 3,900-process box under a full fast tier that passed 4 s; the timeout came back as "no codex thread holds a rollout" (the B-3185 always-failing-degrade shape). The array now carries a non-enumerable `unknown` reason and the degrade is logged (rate-limited); test-discovery-interpret's lsof legs SKIP with that evidence instead of failing parity.
